title=\"Special:MyLanguage/Commons:Vandalism\"\u003Evandalism\u003C/a\u003E to popular pages.\u003C/li\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003EThe reason for protection can be found in the \u003Ca class=\"external class="wbmi-entityview-captionsPanel oo-ui-layout oo-ui-panelLayout oo-ui-panelLayout-framed"><h3 class="wbmi-entityview-captions-header">Captions</h3><div class="wbmi-entityview-caption oo-ui-layout oo-ui-horizontalLayout"><label class="wbmi-language-label oo-ui-widget oo-ui-widget-enabled oo-ui-labelElement-label oo-ui-labelElement oo-ui-labelWidget">English</label><div lang="en" dir="ltr" class="wbmi-caption-value">The Blue Marble is a famous photograph of the Earth taken on December 7, 1972, by the crew of the Apollo 17 spacecraft en route to the Moon at a distance of about 29,400 kilometres (18,300 mi). It shows Africa, Antarctica, and the Arabian Peninsula.</div></div><div style="display:none;" class="wbmi-entityview-caption wbmi-entityview-hideable oo-ui-layout oo-ui-horizontalLayout"><label class="wbmi-language-label oo-ui-widget oo-ui-widget-enabled oo-ui-labelElement-label oo-ui-labelElement oo-ui-labelWidget">German</label><div lang="de" dir="ltr" class="wbmi-caption-value">Die Erde aus dem Weltraum</div></div><div style="display:none;" class="wbmi-entityview-caption wbmi-entityview-hideable oo-ui-layout oo-ui-horizontalLayout"><label class="wbmi-language-label oo-ui-widget oo-ui-widget-enabled oo-ui-labelElement-label oo-ui-labelElement oo-ui-labelWidget">Portuguese</label><div lang="pt" dir="ltr" class="wbmi-caption-value">"The Blue Marble" é uma fotografia famosa da Terra tirada em 1972 pelos tripulantes da Apollo 17.</div></div><div style="display:none;" class="wbmi-entityview-caption wbmi-entityview-hideable oo-ui-layout oo-ui-horizontalLayout"><label class="wbmi-language-label oo-ui-widget oo-ui-widget-enabled oo-ui-labelElement-label oo-ui-labelElement oo-ui-labelWidget">Catalan</label><div lang="ca" dir="ltr" class="wbmi-caption-value">Planeta Terra, vista des de l'Apollo 17 (1972)</div></div><div style="display:none;" class="wbmi-entityview-caption wbmi-entityview-hideable oo-ui-layout oo-ui-horizontalLayout"><label class="wbmi-language-label oo-ui-widget oo-ui-widget-enabled oo-ui-labelElement-label oo-ui-labelElement oo-ui-labelWidget">Malayalam</label><div lang="ml" dir="ltr" class="wbmi-caption-value">ചന്ദ്രനിലേക്കുള്ള യാത്രയ്ക്കിടെ, ഏകദേശം 29,400 കിലോമീറ്റർ (18,300 മൈൽ) അകലെ നിന്ന് അപ്പോളോ-17 ബഹിരാകാശയാത്രികർ 1972 ഡിസംബർ 7 ന് പകർത്തിയ ഭൂമിയുടെ പ്രസിദ്ധമായ ഫോട്ടോയാണ് 'ബ്ലൂ മാർബിൾ'. ഇതിൽ ആഫ്രിക്ക, അന്റാർട്ടിക്ക, അറേബ്യൻ ഉപദ്വീപ് എന്നിവ കാണാം.</div></div><div style="display:none;" class="wbmi-entityview-caption wbmi-entityview-hideable oo-ui-layout oo-ui-horizontalLayout"><label class="wbmi-language-label oo-ui-widget oo-ui-widget-enabled oo-ui-labelElement-label oo-ui-labelElement oo-ui-labelWidget">Chinese</label><div lang="zh" dir="ltr" class="wbmi-caption-value">蓝色弹珠是一张在1972年12月7日由阿波罗17号太空船船员所拍摄的著名地球照片。当时太空船正运行至距离地球29,400公里（18,300英里）之处。</div></div><div style="display:none;" class="wbmi-entityview-caption wbmi-entityview-hideable oo-ui-layout oo-ui-horizontalLayout"><label class="wbmi-language-label oo-ui-widget oo-ui-widget-enabled oo-ui-labelElement-label oo-ui-labelElement oo-ui-labelWidget">Burmese</label><div lang="my" dir="ltr" class="wbmi-caption-value">ကမ္ဘာ</div></div><div style="display:none;" class="wbmi-entityview-caption wbmi-entityview-hideable oo-ui-layout oo-ui-horizontalLayout"><label class="wbmi-language-label oo-ui-widget oo-ui-widget-enabled oo-ui-labelElement-label oo-ui-labelElement oo-ui-labelWidget">Russian</label><div lang="ru" dir="ltr" class="wbmi-caption-value">Голубой мрамор - это знаменитая фотография Земли, сделанная 7 декабря 1972 года экипажем космического корабля «Аполлон-17» на пути к Луне на расстоянии около 29 400 километров. На нем изображены Африка, Антарктида и Аравийский полуостров.</div></div><div style="display:none;" class="wbmi-entityview-caption wbmi-entityview-hideable oo-ui-layout oo-ui-horizontalLayout"><label 세 번째 행성 지구</div></div><div style="display:none;" class="wbmi-entityview-caption wbmi-entityview-hideable oo-ui-layout oo-ui-horizontalLayout"><label class="wbmi-language-label oo-ui-widget oo-ui-widget-enabled oo-ui-labelElement-label oo-ui-labelElement oo-ui-labelWidget">Slovak</label><div lang="sk" dir="ltr" class="wbmi-caption-value">Modrá Gulička je známa fotografia zemegule मार्बल (निळी गोटी): अपोलो १७ अंतराळयानातून ७ डिसेंबर १९७२ रोजी २९००० किमी अंतरावरून</div></div><div style="display:none;" class="wbmi-entityview-caption wbmi-entityview-hideable oo-ui-layout oo-ui-horizontalLayout"><label class="wbmi-language-label oo-ui-widget oo-ui-widget-enabled oo-ui-labelElement-label oo-ui-labelElement oo-ui-labelWidget">Hindi</label><div lang="hi" dir="ltr" class="wbmi-caption-value">ब्लू मार्बल 7 दिसंबर 1972 को अपोलो 17 अंतरिक्ष यान के चालक दल द्वारा चंद्रमा पर लगभग 29,400 किलोमीटर (18,300 मील) की दूरी पर पृथ्वी की एक प्रसिद्ध तस्वीर है।  यह अफ्रीका, अंटार्कटिका और अरब प्रायद्वीप को दर्शाता है</div></div><div style="display:none;" class="wbmi-entityview-caption wbmi-entityview-hideable oo-ui-layout oo-ui-horizontalLayout"><label class="wbmi-language-label oo-ui-widget oo-ui-widget-enabled oo-ui-labelElement-label oo-ui-labelElement oo-ui-labelWidget">Ukrainian</label><div lang="uk" dir="ltr" class="wbmi-caption-value">Блакитна іграшкова куля — відома фотографія Землі, зроблена 7 грудня 1972 року командою космічного апарату «Аполлон-17».</div></div><div style="display:none;" class="wbmi-entityview-caption wbmi-entityview-hideable oo-ui-layout oo-ui-horizontalLayout"><label class="wbmi-language-label oo-ui-widget oo-ui-widget-enabled oo-ui-labelElement-label oo-ui-labelElement oo-ui-labelWidget">Persian</label><div lang="fa" dir="rtl" class="wbmi-caption-value">نگاره‌ای از کرهٔ زمین که در ۷ دسامبر سال ۱۹۷۲ م. از فاصلهٔ ۲۹٬۰۰۰ کیلومتری گرفته شده‌است</div></div><div style="display:none;" class="wbmi-entityview-caption wbmi-entityview-hideable oo-ui-layout oo-ui-horizontalLayout"><label class="wbmi-language-label oo-ui-widget oo-ui-widget-enabled oo-ui-labelElement-label oo-ui-labelElement oo-ui-labelWidget">Bangla</label><div lang="bn" dir="ltr" class="wbmi-caption-value">ব্লু মার্বেল পৃথিবীর একটি বিখ্যাত ছবি যা ৭ ই ডিসেম্বর, ১৯৭২ সালে, অ্যাপোলো ১৭ মহাকাশযানের ক্রুরা প্রায় ২৯,০০০ কিলোমিটার (১৮,০০০ মাইল) দূরত্ব থেকে তোলা হয়েছিল। এই ছবিটিতে আফ্রিকা, অ্যান্টার্কটিকা এবং আরব উপদ্বীপ দেখা যায়।</div></div><div style="display:none;" class="wbmi-entityview-caption wbmi-entityview-hideable oo-ui-layout oo-ui-horizontalLayout"><label class="wbmi-language-label oo-ui-widget oo-ui-widget-enabled التقُطت الصورة في 7 كانون الأول/ديسمبر 1972 بواسطةِ طاقم مركبة الفضاء الأمريكيّة أبولو 17 عند مغادرتهم مدار الأرض للقمر من على بعد حوالي 29.000 كيلومتر عن سطح الأرض</div></div><div style="display:none;" class="wbmi-entityview-caption wbmi-entityview-hideable oo-ui-layout oo-ui-horizontalLayout"><label class="wbmi-language-label oo-ui-widget oo-ui-widget-enabled style="display:none;" class="wbmi-entityview-caption wbmi-entityview-hideable oo-ui-layout oo-ui-horizontalLayout"><label class="wbmi-language-label oo-ui-widget oo-ui-widget-enabled oo-ui-labelElement-label oo-ui-labelElement oo-ui-labelWidget">Traditional Chinese</label><div lang="zh-hant" dir="ltr" class="wbmi-caption-value">“蓝色大理石”是第一张地球全景照片，是1972年阿波罗17号宇航员在飞往月球的途中拍摄的。</div></div><div style="display:none;" class="wbmi-entityview-caption wbmi-entityview-hideable oo-ui-layout dir="ltr" class="wbmi-caption-value">Mavi küre (The Blue Marble) 7 Aralık 1972 günü o sırada ay görevinde olan Apollo 17 mürettebatı tarafından 29400 kilometre uzaklıkta iken çekilmiştir. Fotoğrafta Afrika, Antarktika, Arab yarımadası net bir şekilde görülmektedir.</div></div><div style="display:none;" class="wbmi-entityview-caption wbmi-entityview-hideable oo-ui-layout oo-ui-horizontalLayout"><label class="wbmi-language-label oo-ui-widget oo-ui-widget-enabled oo-ui-labelElement-label oo-ui-labelElement oo-ui-labelWidget">Hebrew</label><div lang="he" dir="rtl" class="wbmi-caption-value">כדור הארץ</div></div><div style="display:none;" class="wbmi-entityview-caption wbmi-entityview-hideable oo-ui-layout oo-ui-horizontalLayout"><label class="wbmi-language-label oo-ui-widget oo-ui-widget-enabled oo-ui-labelElement-label oo-ui-labelElement oo-ui-labelWidget">Serbian</label><div lang="sr" dir="ltr" class="wbmi-caption-value">Плави кликер је позната фотографија Земље, сликана 7. Децембра, 1972. године. Слику је реализовала посада свемирске експедиције Аполона 17. На слици су приказани континенти Африке, Антсрктика, и арапско полуострво.</div></div><div style="display:none;" class="wbmi-entityview-caption wbmi-entityview-hideable oo-ui-layout oo-ui-horizontalLayout"><label class="wbmi-language-label oo-ui-widget oo-ui-widget-enabled oo-ui-labelElement-label oo-ui-labelElement oo-ui-labelWidget">Belarusian</label><div lang="be" dir="ltr" class="wbmi-caption-value">Фотаздымак Зямлі з космасу</div></div><div style="display:none;" class="wbmi-entityview-caption wbmi-entityview-hideable oo-ui-layout oo-ui-horizontalLayout"><label class="wbmi-language-label oo-ui-widget oo-ui-widget-enabled oo-ui-labelElement-label oo-ui-labelElement oo-ui-labelWidget">Thai</label><div lang="th" dir="ltr" class="wbmi-caption-value">รูปภาพจำลองของโลก</div></div><div style="display:none;" class="wbmi-entityview-caption wbmi-entityview-hideable oo-ui-layout oo-ui-horizontalLayout"><label class="wbmi-language-label oo-ui-widget oo-ui-widget-enabled oo-ui-labelElement-label oo-ui-labelElement oo-ui-labelWidget">Spanish</label><div lang="es" dir="ltr" class="wbmi-caption-value">Foto del planeta tierra desde el espacio que nos rodea(alturas de nuestro planeta)</div></div><div style="display:none;" class="wbmi-entityview-caption wbmi-entityview-hideable oo-ui-layout oo-ui-horizontalLayout"><label class="wbmi-language-label oo-ui-widget oo-ui-widget-enabled oo-ui-labelElement-label oo-ui-labelElement oo-ui-labelWidget">Gujarati</label><div lang="gu" dir="ltr" class="wbmi-caption-value">પૃથ્વીનું અવકાશથી લીધેલ ચિત્ર</div></div></div><div id="mw-imagepage-content"><div class="mw-content-ltr mw-parser-output" lang="en" dir="ltr">

<tr>
<td id="fileinfotpl_desc" class="fileinfo-paramfield" lang="en">Description</td>
<td>
<div class="description">
<div class="description ar" dir="rtl" lang="ar"><span class="language ar" title="Arabic"><b>العربية: </b></span> صورة الگُلَّة (البلية) الزرقاء الشهيرة التي تعتبر أول صورة لمنظر الأرض الكامل. إلتُقطت الصورة في 7 كانون الأوَّل (ديسمبر) 1972 بواسطة طاقم مركبة الفضاء الأمريكيَّة أپولو 17 عند مغادرتهم مدار الأرض لِلقمر من على بعد حوالي 29,400 كيلومتر عن سطح الأرض. إلتقط الطاقم صورة الكوكب الأزرق بِشكلٍ مُتقنٍ حيثُ كانت الشمس من ورائهم وهو ما سمح بِإضائة مُمتازة لِكوكب الأرض، حيثُ تظهر أفريقيا، والقارَّة القطبيَّة الجنوبيَّة (أنتاركتيكا) وشبه الجزيرة العربيَّة.</div>
<div class="description de" dir="ltr" lang="de"><span class="language de" title="German"><b>Deutsch: </b></span> „<a href="https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Blue_Marble" class="extiw" title="de:Blue Marble">Blue Marble</a>“, die während des Fluges von <a href="https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Apollo_17" class="extiw" title="de:Apollo 17">Apollo 17</a> zum Mond am 7. Dezember 1972 entstandene Fotoaufnahme von der Erde</div>
<div class="description en" dir="ltr" lang="en"><span class="language en" title="English"><b>English: </b></span> "<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Blue_Marble" class="extiw" title="en:The Blue Marble">The Blue Marble</a>" is a famous photograph of the Earth taken on December 7, 1972, by the crew of the <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Apollo_17" class="extiw" title="en:Apollo 17">Apollo 17</a> spacecraft en route to the Moon at a distance of about 29,400 kilometres (18,300&nbsp;mi). It shows <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Africa" class="extiw" title="en:Africa">Africa</a>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Antarctica" class="extiw" title="en:Antarctica">Antarctica</a>, and the <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabian_Peninsula" class="extiw" title="en:Arabian Peninsula">Arabian Peninsula</a>.</div>
<div class="description es" dir="ltr" lang="es"><span class="language es" title="Spanish"><b>Español: </b></span> "<a href="https://es.wikipedia.org/wiki/La_canica_azul" class="extiw" title="es:La canica azul">La canica azul</a>". Imagen de la Tierra tomada desde el <a href="https://es.wikipedia.org/wiki/Apolo_17" class="extiw" title="es:Apolo 17">Apollo 17</a>.</div>
<div class="description fa" dir="rtl" lang="fa"><span class="language fa" title="Persian"><b>فارسی: </b></span> «<a href="https://fa.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D8%AA%DB%8C%D9%84%D9%87_%D8%A2%D8%A8%DB%8C" class="extiw" title="fa:تیله آبی">تیلهٔ آبی</a>» تصویر معروفی از زمین که در ۷ دسامبر ۱۹۷۲ توسط خدمهٔ فضاپیمای <a href="https://fa.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D8%A2%D9%BE%D9%88%D9%84%D9%88_%DB%B1%DB%B7" class="extiw" title="fa:آپولو ۱۷">آپولو ۱۷</a> در مسیر ماه در حدود ۲٬۹۰۰ کیلومتری زمین گرفته شده است و قسمت‌هایی از <a href="https://fa.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D8%A2%D9%81%D8%B1%DB%8C%D9%82%D8%A7" class="extiw" title="fa:آفریقا">آفریقا</a>، <a href="https://fa.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D8%AC%D9%86%D9%88%D8%A8%DA%AF%D8%A7%D9%86" class="extiw" title="fa:جنوبگان">جنوبگان</a> و <a href="https://fa.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D8%B4%D8%A8%D9%87_%D8%AC%D8%B2%DB%8C%D8%B1%D9%87_%D8%B9%D8%B1%D8%A8" class="extiw" title="fa:شبه جزیره عرب">شبه جزیرهٔ عرب</a> را نشان می‌دهد.</div>
<div class="description fi" dir="ltr" lang="fi"><span class="language fi" title="Finnish"><b>Suomi: </b></span> <a href="https://fi.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sininen_marmorikuula" class="extiw" title="fi:Sininen marmorikuula">Sininen marmorikuula</a>.</div>
<div class="description fr" dir="ltr" lang="fr"><span class="language fr" title="French"><b>Français&nbsp;: </b></span> «<a href="https://fr.wikipedia.org/wiki/La_bille_bleue" class="extiw" title="fr:La bille bleue">La bille bleue</a>»&nbsp;: Photo de l'Afrique, de l'Antarctique et de la péninsule Arabique prise en route pour la lune par <a href="https://fr.wikipedia.org/wiki/Harrison_Schmitt" class="extiw" title="fr:Harrison Schmitt">Harrison Schmitt</a> ou <a href="https://fr.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ronald_Evans" class="extiw" title="fr:Ronald Evans">Ron Evans</a> lors de la mission <a href="https://fr.wikipedia.org/wiki/Apollo_17" class="extiw" title="fr:Apollo 17">Apollo 17</a> le 7 décembre 1972. Ce vol a été le dernier à quitter l'orbite terrestre, et le seul au cours duquel un géologue, Harrison Schmitt, s'est rendu sur la lune.</div>
<div class="description hi" dir="ltr" lang="hi"><span class="language hi" title="Hindi"><b>हिन्दी: </b></span> "<a href="https://hi.wikipedia.org/wiki/%E0%A4%A6_%E0%A4%AC%E0%A5%8D%E0%A4%B2%E0%A5%82_%E0%A4%AE%E0%A4%BE%E0%A4%B0%E0%A5%8D%E0%A4%AC%E0%A4%B2" class="extiw" title="hi:द ब्लू मार्बल">द ब्लू मार्बल</a>" लगभग 29,400 kilometres (18,300&nbsp;mi) की ऊँचाई पर चन्द्रमा की यात्रा पर गये अंतरिक्षयान <a href="https://hi.wikipedia.org/wiki/%E0%A4%85%E0%A4%AA%E0%A5%8B%E0%A4%B2%E0%A5%8B_%E0%A5%A7%E0%A5%AD" class="extiw" title="hi:अपोलो १७">अपोलो १७</a> के सदस्यों द्वारा दिसम्बर २००७ को लिया गया प्रसिद्ध चित्र है। इसमें <a href="https://hi.wikipedia.org/wiki/%E0%A4%85%E0%A4%AB%E0%A4%BC%E0%A5%8D%E0%A4%B0%E0%A5%80%E0%A4%95%E0%A4%BE" class="extiw" title="hi:अफ़्रीका">अफ़्रीका</a>, <a href="https://hi.wikipedia.org/wiki/%E0%A4%85%E0%A4%82%E0%A4%9F%E0%A4%BE%E0%A4%B0%E0%A5%8D%E0%A4%95%E0%A4%9F%E0%A4%BF%E0%A4%95%E0%A4%BE" class="extiw" title="hi:अंटार्कटिका">अंटार्कटिका</a> और <a href="https://hi.wikipedia.org/wiki/%E0%A4%85%E0%A4%B0%E0%A4%AC%E0%A5%80_%E0%A4%AA%E0%A5%8D%E0%A4%B0%E0%A4%BE%E0%A4%AF%E0%A4%A6%E0%A5%8D%E0%A4%B5%E0%A5%80%E0%A4%AA" class="extiw" title="hi:अरबी प्रायद्वीप">अरबी प्रायद्वीप</a> दिखाई दे रहे हैं।</div>
<div class="description it" dir="ltr" lang="it"><span class="language it" title="Italian"><b>Italiano: </b></span> Famosa fotografia della <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Terra" class="extiw" title="en:Terra">Terra</a> ripresa il 7 dicembre 1972 dall'equipaggio della missione <a href="https://it.wikipedia.org/wiki/Apollo_17" class="extiw" title="it:Apollo 17">Apollo 17</a> diretta verso la Luna, da una distanza di circa 29.400 km.</div>
<div class="description hu" dir="ltr" lang="hu"><span class="language hu" title="Hungarian"><b>Magyar: </b></span> A „<a href="https://hu.wikipedia.org/wiki/K%C3%A9k_%C3%BCveggoly%C3%B3" class="extiw" title="hu:Kék üveggolyó">Kék üveggolyó</a>”, egy híres fénykép a <a href="https://hu.wikipedia.org/wiki/F%C3%B6ld" class="extiw" title="hu:Föld">Földr</a>ől, amelyet a <a href="https://hu.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hold" class="extiw" title="hu:Hold">Hold</a> felé tartó <a href="https://hu.wikipedia.org/wiki/Apollo%E2%80%9317" class="extiw" title="hu:Apollo–17">Apollo–17</a> űrhajó legénysége készített 1972. december 7-én, mintegy 29 ezer kilométer távolságból. Jól látható rajta egész <a href="https://hu.wikipedia.org/wiki/Afrika" class="extiw" title="hu:Afrika">Afrika</a>, az <a href="https://hu.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arab-f%C3%A9lsziget" class="extiw" title="hu:Arab-félsziget">Arab-félsziget</a> és az <a href="https://hu.wikipedia.org/wiki/Antarktisz" class="extiw" title="hu:Antarktisz">Antarktisz</a>.</div>
<div class="description mk" dir="ltr" lang="mk"><span class="language mk" title="Macedonian"><b>Македонски: </b></span> „<a href="https://mk.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D0%A1%D0%B8%D0%BD%D0%B0%D1%82%D0%B0_%D1%9F%D0%B0%D0%BC%D0%BB%D0%B8%D1%98%D0%B0" class="extiw" title="mk:Сината џамлија">Сината џамлија</a>“ е позната фотографија на Земјата направена на 7 декември 1972 од екипажот на вселенското летало <a href="https://mk.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D0%90%D0%BF%D0%BE%D0%BB%D0%BE_17" class="extiw" title="mk:Аполо 17">Аполо 17</a> патувајќи кон Месечината. Сликана е на растојание од 29.400 километри, а на неа се гледаат <a href="https://mk.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D0%90%D1%84%D1%80%D0%B8%D0%BA%D0%B0" class="extiw" title="mk:Африка">Африка</a>, <a href="https://mk.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D0%90%D0%BD%D1%82%D0%B0%D1%80%D0%BA%D1%82%D0%B8%D0%BA" class="extiw" title="mk:Антарктик">Антарктикот</a> и <a href="https://mk.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D0%90%D1%80%D0%B0%D0%BF%D1%81%D0%BA%D0%B8_%D0%9F%D0%BE%D0%BB%D1%83%D0%BE%D1%81%D1%82%D1%80%D0%BE%D0%B2" class="extiw" title="mk:Арапски Полуостров">Арапскиот Полуостров</a>.</div>
<div class="description pt" dir="ltr" lang="pt"><span class="language pt" title="Portuguese"><b>Português: </b></span> <a href="https://pt.wikipedia.org/wiki/A_Bolinha_Azul" class="extiw" title="pt:A Bolinha Azul">A Bolinha Azul</a> é uma fotografia famosa da Terra tirada a 7 de dezembro de 1972 durante a missão <a href="https://pt.wikipedia.org/wiki/Apollo_17" class="extiw" title="pt:Apollo 17">Apollo 17</a>, quando se encontrava a caminho da Lua e a 29,400 quilómetros da Terra.</div>
<div class="description ru" dir="ltr" lang="ru"><span class="language ru" title="Russian"><b>Русский: </b></span> <a href="https://ru.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Blue_Marble" class="extiw" title="ru:The Blue Marble">Вид Земли</a> с космического корабля <a href="https://ru.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D0%90%D0%BF%D0%BE%D0%BB%D0%BB%D0%BE%D0%BD-17" class="extiw" title="ru:Аполлон-17">Аполлон-17</a>. 7 декабря 1972 года.</div>
<div class="description uk" dir="ltr" lang="uk"><span class="language uk" title="Ukrainian"><b>Українська: </b></span> <a href="https://uk.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D0%91%D0%BB%D0%B0%D0%BA%D0%B8%D1%82%D0%BD%D0%B0_%D1%96%D0%B3%D1%80%D0%B0%D1%88%D0%BA%D0%BE%D0%B2%D0%B0_%D0%BA%D1%83%D0%BB%D1%8F" class="extiw" title="uk:Блакитна іграшкова куля">Блакитна іграшкова куля</a> — відома фотографія Землі, зроблена 7 грудня 1972 року командою космічного апарату «<a href="https://uk.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D0%90%D0%BF%D0%BE%D0%BB%D0%BB%D0%BE%D0%BD-17" class="extiw" title="uk:Аполлон-17">Аполлон-17</a>».</div>
<div class="description zh-hans" dir="ltr" lang="zh-hans"><span class="language zh-hans" title="Simplified Chinese"><b>中文（简体）：</b></span>​<a href="https://zh.wikipedia.org/wiki/%E8%93%9D%E8%89%B2%E5%BC%B9%E7%8F%A0" class="extiw" title="zh:蓝色弹珠">蓝色弹珠</a> 是一张在1972年12月7日由阿波罗17号太空船船员所拍摄的著名地球照片。当时太空船正运行至距离地球29,400公里（18,300英里）之处。</div>
<div class="description ja" dir="ltr" lang="ja"><span class="language ja" title="Japanese"><b>日本語:</b></span> <a href="https://ja.wikipedia.org/wiki/%E3%82%B6%E3%83%BB%E3%83%96%E3%83%AB%E3%83%BC%E3%83%BB%E3%83%9E%E3%83%BC%E3%83%96%E3%83%AB" class="extiw" title="ja:ザ・ブルー・マーブル">ザ・ブルー・マーブル</a>は、月へ向かう<a href="https://ja.wikipedia.org/wiki/%E3%82%A2%E3%83%9D%E3%83%AD17%E5%8F%B7" class="extiw" title="ja:アポロ17号">アポロ17号</a>のクルーが1972年12月7日に撮影した有名な地球の写真。29,400km離れた距離から撮影された。<a href="https://ja.wikipedia.org/wiki/%E3%82%A2%E3%83%95%E3%83%AA%E3%82%AB" class="extiw" title="ja:アフリカ">アフリカ</a>、<a href="https://ja.wikipedia.org/wiki/%E3%82%A2%E3%83%A9%E3%83%93%E3%82%A2%E5%8D%8A%E5%B3%B6" class="extiw" title="ja:アラビア半島">アラビア半島</a>、<a href="https://ja.wikipedia.org/wiki/%E5%8D%97%E6%A5%B5%E5%A4%A7%E9%99%B8" class="extiw" title="ja:南極大陸">南極大陸</a>、<a href="https://ja.wikipedia.org/wiki/%E3%82%A4%E3%83%B3%E3%83%89%E6%B4%8B" class="extiw" title="ja:インド洋">インド洋</a>などを捉えている。</div>
<div class="description ko" dir="ltr" lang="ko"><span class="language ko" title="Korean"><b>한국어: </b></span> <a href="https://ko.wikipedia.org/wiki/%ED%91%B8%EB%A5%B8_%EA%B5%AC%EC%8A%AC" class="extiw" title="ko:푸른 구슬">푸른 구슬</a>은 1972년 12월 7일 <a href="https://ko.wikipedia.org/wiki/%EC%95%84%ED%8F%B4%EB%A1%9C_17%ED%98%B8" class="extiw" title="ko:아폴로 17호">아폴로 17호</a> 우주선 승무원들이 약 29,400km에서 달로 향하던 중 촬영한 유명한 지구 사진이다. <a href="https://ko.wikipedia.org/wiki/%EC%95%84%ED%94%84%EB%A6%AC%EC%B9%B4" class="extiw" title="ko:아프리카">아프리카</a>, <a href="https://ko.wikipedia.org/wiki/%EB%82%A8%EA%B7%B9" class="extiw" title="ko:남극">남극</a>, <a href="https://ko.wikipedia.org/wiki/%EC%95%84%EB%9D%BC%EB%B9%84%EC%95%84%EB%B0%98%EB%8F%84" class="extiw" title="ko:아라비아반도">아라비아반도</a>를 보여준다.</div>
<div class="description cs" dir="ltr" lang="cs"><span class="language cs" title="Czech"><b>Čeština: </b></span> <a href="https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Modr%C3%A1_sklen%C4%9Bnka" class="extiw" title="cs:Modrá skleněnka">Modrá skleněnka</a> je fotografie planety Země, kterou 7. prosince 1972 pořídila posádka <a href="https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Apollo_17" class="extiw" title="cs:Apollo 17">Apolla 17</a> ze vzdálenosti zhruba 29 400 kilometrů.</div></div></td>
</tr>

<td>This file was the <b><a href="/wiki/Commons:Picture_of_the_day" title="Commons:Picture of the day">picture of the day</a></b> on <a href="/wiki/Template:Potd/2006-08#20" title="Template:Potd/2006-08">August 20, 2006</a>.<br>This formerly was a <b>featured picture</b> on Wikimedia Commons (<a href="/wiki/Commons:Featured_pictures" title="Commons:Featured pictures">Featured pictures</a>) and was considered one of the finest images. See its nomination <a href="/wiki/Commons:Featured_picture_candidates/removal/File:The_Earth_seen_from_Apollo_17.jpg" title="Commons:Featured picture candidates/removal/File:The Earth seen from Apollo 17.jpg">here</a>.<br>This image has been assessed under the <a href="/wiki/Commons:Valued_image_criteria" title="Commons:Valued image criteria">valued image criteria</a> and is considered the most <b><a href="/wiki/Commons:Valued_images" title="Commons:Valued images">valued image</a></b> on Commons within the scope <b>Earth from space</b>. <div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Additional_information">Additional information</h2></div>
<p>Source: <a rel="nofollow" class="external free" data-mw-original-href="http://nssdc.gsfc.nasa.gov/imgcat/html/object_page/a17_h_148_22727.html" href="https://nssdc.gsfc.nasa.gov/imgcat/html/object_page/a17_h_148_22727.html">http://nssdc.gsfc.nasa.gov/imgcat/html/object_page/a17_h_148_22727.html</a>

<table class="cquote" style="margin:auto; border-collapse: collapse; border: none; background-color: transparent; color: inherit; width: auto;">
<tbody><tr>
<td width="20" valign="top" style="border:none; color:#B2B7F2;font-size:35px;font-family: 'Times New Roman', serif; font-weight: bold; text-align: left; padding: 10px 10px;">“
</td>
<td valign="top" style="border: none; padding: 4px 10px;">Earth - Apollo 17
<p>Full Earth showing Africa and Antarctica
</p><p>Apollo 17 hand-held Hasselblad picture of the full Earth. This picture was taken on 7 December 1972, as the spacecraft traveled to the moon as the last of the Apollo missions. A remarkably cloud-free Africa is at upper left, stretching down to the center of the image. Saudi Arabia is visible at the top of the disk and Antarctica and the south pole are at the bottom. Asia is on the horizon is at upper right. The Earth is 12,740&nbsp;km (7,920&nbsp;mi) in diameter. (Apollo 17, AS17-148-22727) 
</p>
<div class="poem">
